- **clauz3**: Use when an agent needs to act in the real world — send mail, edit a file, fetch a URL, move money, run a lab step, modify any external system — and finds that no direct tool exists for it. If the project ships a clauz3 trusted layer, that IS the tool, but reached only by writing a small Python program against trusted modules, attaching guarantees the user will review, and passing it through `clauz3 prove` / `clauz3 run`. This skill is how to pass through that gate. ([normalform-ai/clauz3](https://github.com/normalform-ai/clauz3/tree/HEAD/skills/clauz3) · ★5)
- **clauz3-authoring**: Design or extend a Clauz3 trusted library when agents need capabilities or safety contracts that existing libraries do not provide.
